[22:0x CT 09-28] PHASE A census (P8 run before building): convb_build binds all 9 READY mobs (unbound: turtle tail — no Patrix part; tropical A left/right_fin0 — type-B-only bones). TWO NEW FINDINGS the 21:18 preview did not show (no ground reference in the tiles): (1) PLACEMENT — turtle new model y -12.4..3.0 (12 px under the ground), axolotl y -21.8..-7.6 (22 px under), dolphin 5-7 px behind vanilla. Mechanism: OptiFine hangs each JEM part model under the VANILLA part (world = V + R_v (p - D)); the declared-pivot rule assumes V = D. FreshLX moves the vanilla part on purpose in 3 READY mobs (turtle body ty 17 tz -1; axolotl body t* = 0; salmon body_front tz 0) and the dolphin template body pivot is (0,0,-3) vs declared (0,3,2). Exact rule reproduces vanilla placement: turtle shell y 3..9 z -9..13 (vanilla 3..9, -7..13); axolotl body2 pivot y 4 (vanilla 4); dolphin z -13.8..25.3 (vanilla -13..25). Equines unaffected (V-D <= 0.4 px). (2) FRAMES — animated bones under a 90 deg parent (turtle head; wolf head/head2/mane/tail under body 90) would take our animations in a rotated frame -> frame-alignment bones.
